| Google Sheets-first · CFBD data in, opponent-adjusted ratings out, your OddsLogic/Circa lines for edges · no picks sold here, just process |  |  |
| PHASE 5.1 STATE (scope change): FBS-vs-FCS games are permanently non-actionable - status "FCS — NO PLAY", no market line required, no spread/total/edge/label/confidence calculated, excluded from effective-game and weekly rating-movement calculations. Their schedule entries and final scores are still tracked for reference. NDSU and Sacramento State remain normal FBS-reclassifying teams (unaffected; existing transitional safeguards intact). SP+, FPI, TeamRankings preseason ratings loaded for all 138 FBS teams (blend weight 0.70). Still blocked/unpopulated: TTW independent 2025 prior (see CHANGELOG), VSiN (user-supplied, not provided), QB values (see unresolved-QB report), market lines, in-season statistics. |  |  |
| SHEETS SETUP: after import, set File \> Settings \> Time zone = America/New\_York (see DICTIONARY checklist). The .xlsx cannot carry this setting. |  |  |
| WEEKLY ROUTINE (target 25–40 min). Status lights update live. |  |  |
| Step | What to do | Status |
| 1 | Set SETTINGS -\> Season, Current week, As-of date | — set week + as-of date |
| 2 | Pull the CFBD schedule + season-to-date stats files (recipes in DICTIONARY) | — |
| 3 | Paste stats file into IMPORT STATS at A6 (whole file, replaces old) | — (preseason: priors drive ratings) |
| 4 | Paste schedule file into IMPORT SCHEDULE at A6 | 888 games loaded |
| 5 | Confirm QB VALUES — active starter, delta, confidence, Reviewed-for-season = current, fresh date | 39 teams QB UNCERTAIN |
| 6 | Enter lines in MARKET LINES from OddsLogic/Circa screenshots (favorite + POSITIVE spread + total + date) | Missing: 761 lines |
| 7 | Log injuries/situational spots in ADJUSTMENTS (reason required) | OK |
| 8 | Clear DATA QUALITY, then work the DASHBOARD by Priority | OK — nothing blocked |
| A | Structural audit (always on) — must be OK before trusting any status above | OK — all invariants pass |
| SIGN CONVENTION — worked example (this trips everyone once) |  |  |
| • Market: Georgia -7.5 at home -\> enter Favorite=UGA, Spread=7.5 -\> sheet stores market home spread = -7.5 |  |  |
| • Model: Final margin = +9.0 (home wins by 9) -\> model spread displays UGA -9.0 |  |  |
| • Spread edge = Final margin + market home spread = 9.0 + (-7.5) = +1.5 -\> positive = value on HOME at -7.5 |  |  |
| • If Final margin were +5.0: edge = 5.0 + (-7.5) = -2.5 -\> negative = value on AWAY at +7.5 |  |  |
| • Bare 'Miami' is rejected in manual entry — use MIA (ACC) or M-OH (Miami OH). Schedule imports resolve 'Miami' automatically via conference context. |  |  |

| PERMANENT DISCLAIMER |  |  |
| Labels identify model-versus-market divergence, not guaranteed betting value. Early-season college-football ratings carry substantial uncertainty. |  |  |

| TAB MAP |  |  |
| DASHBOARD | This week at a glance, filter by Week / Major scope / Status / Label |  |
| ENGINE | Every game, every bridge step from ratings to final number |  |
| MARKET LINES | OddsLogic/Circa entry (favorite + positive spread); reference columns for both books |  |
| ADJUSTMENTS | Every subjective point you add — documented, dated, reversible, expiring |  |
| QB VALUES | Baseline-delta QB system — only deltas move projections |  |
| TEAM RATINGS | Effective ratings + fade + HFA table + override column + transition review |  |
| DATA QUALITY | What is blocked and why, with how-to-clear instructions |  |
| SETTINGS | Every weight, threshold, fade table and scope list |  |
| IMPORT tabs | Paste zones for the CFBD schedule and stats files |  |
| Hidden: TEAM MAP/CLEAN/CALC | The pipeline — unhide to audit, never edit |  |
| Hidden: PRESEASON/FCS TIERS | Prior blend (Phase 4) and FCS framework |  |
| Hidden: HISTORY/BACKTEST/AUDIT/DICTIONARY/CHANGELOG | Receipts: snapshots, tests, invariants, definitions, versions |  |

| DATA QUALITY — what is blocked and why |  |  |
| Hard blocks suppress projections; warnings reduce confidence. A failed lookup never defaults to READY. Suppression priority: BLOCKED -\> FCS — NO PLAY -\> PENDING LINE -\> STALE LINE -\> QB UNCERTAIN -\> TRANSITION UNCERTAIN -\> DATA INCOMPLETE -\> READY. FCS — NO PLAY (v0.5.1) is permanent, not a data gap to fix. |  |  |

| Check | Count | How to clear |
| Games loaded | 888 |  |
| BLOCKED (hard) | 0 | Filter ENGINE col AI = BLOCKED; reasons in col AH |
| PENDING LINE | 761 | Enter favorite + positive spread in MARKET LINES |
| STALE LINE | 0 | Re-enter from a fresh OddsLogic/Circa screenshot |
| QB UNCERTAIN | 0 | Fix delta/confidence/reviewed-season in QB VALUES |
| TRANSITION UNCERTAIN | 0 | Reclassifier not yet review-cleared |
| DATA INCOMPLETE | 0 | Week/date missing on the imported row |
| READY | 0 |  |
| Market-line rows with flags | 0 | See MARKET LINES col Q |
| Adjustment rows with flags | 0 | Reason missing / non-numeric / oversized |
| Stats rows UNMATCHED | 0 | Team name not in IMPORT alias table |
| Duplicate GameIDs in schedule | 0 | Hard block on affected games |
| Import rows failing team resolution (BLOCK) | 0 | FBS conference but unknown team — fix alias in TEAM MAP + CHANGELOG |
| Teams SINGLE-SOURCE / LOW CONFIDENCE | 0 | Documented missing sources in PRESEASON |
| AUDIT invariants failing (workbook-level) | 0 | Any nonzero value blocks go-live; open AUDIT for the failing row and TEAM MAP col J for alias diagnostics |
| FCS — NO PLAY (reference only, not a data-quality issue) | 127 | Permanent by design (v0.5.1 scope change) - not clearable, not counted as a block |

| :-: | :-: |
| DICTIONARY — definitions, sources, import recipes, methodology notes |  |
|  |  |
|  |  |
| Term | Definition / procedure |
| IMPORT SCHEDULE column order (fixed) | id, season, week, start\_date, neutral\_site, away\_team, away\_conference, home\_team, home\_conference, away\_points, home\_points, completed, venue, notes. Source: CollegeFootballData /games endpoint (free API key at collegefootballdata.com) exported to CSV in this column order; manual CSV paste is the always-available fallback. Cross-check weeks/neutral sites vs FBSchedules/ESPN. |
| IMPORT STATS column order (fixed) | team, games, off\_epa\_play, def\_epa\_play, off\_success\_rate, def\_success\_rate, off\_ppp, def\_ppp, pace. Source: CFBD/cfbfastR season-to-date team advanced stats. Basic-stat fallback: leave advanced fields blank — the engine degrades to the available component and DATA QUALITY warns; it never fakes a value. |
| TTW independent 2025 prior (PRESEASON col L) | Built in Phase 4 from verified 2025 CFBD/cfbfastR data: single-pass opponent-adjusted scoring margin (per-game cap ±28, venue-adjusted, recency-weighted) blended with EPA/play, success rate and points-per-play composites; normalized to points vs average FBS. Full parameter set documented here when populated. NOT tuned against betting results. NOT an SP+ derivative — if it cannot be built reliably, the build stops and reports the limitation. |
| Source correlation risk | SP+, FPI and TeamRankings are correlated model-family ratings with overlapping inputs; the TTW prior is the only fully independent blend member; VSiN is market-adjacent. Interpreting agreement among SP+/FPI/TR as three independent confirmations overstates certainty. |
| Normalization | Mean-centering: each source's normalized value = raw minus the average of that source's available raws, expressed in points vs average FBS. Missing sources renormalize the remaining weights; they are never zeroes. |
| Effective games played | Completed FBS-opponent game = 1.0; completed FCS-opponent game = 0.5; bye/cancelled = 0. Drives the prior fade (SETTINGS table, linearly interpolated, 10% floor). |
| Opponent adjustment (in-season) | Single-pass: each game performance = capped venue-adjusted margin minus the opponent's PRESEASON prior anchor. Documented simplification to avoid circular references; iterative refinement is a Phase 7 item and any change is CHANGELOG-versioned. |
| Statuses (suppression priority) | BLOCKED \> FCS — NO PLAY \> PENDING LINE \> STALE LINE \> QB UNCERTAIN \> TRANSITION UNCERTAIN \> DATA INCOMPLETE \> READY. A failed lookup can never produce READY. FCS — NO PLAY (added v0.5.1) is permanent for any FBS-vs-FCS game - no market line, spread, total, edge, label, or confidence score is ever calculated for it, and it is excluded from effective-game/rating-movement calculations. Schedule and final score are still tracked for reference. |
| Labels | LEAN \|edge\| 1.0–1.49 · INVESTIGATE 1.5+ · BET 3.0+ only when toggle=Y AND status=READY AND no transitional flag. FBS-vs-FCS games never reach a label (status is always FCS — NO PLAY, never READY). Totals use 2x the spread thresholds (documented choice; revisit in Phase 7). Labels = model-vs-market divergence, not proven profitability. |
| BET gates | Toggle=Y; Data Quality READY; fresh line; sufficient confidence; QBs reviewed, not Low; adjustment limits respected; no transitional block; no missing rating/schedule data. FBS-vs-FCS games are categorically excluded (status FCS — NO PLAY, never READY - see v0.5.1). Reclassifier BET block clears only manually after \>=4 FBS games + QB reviewed + data complete + CHANGELOG entry. |
| NO AUTOMATIC RECLASSIFIER UPGRADE guard | For NDSU/Sacramento State: positive 2025 common-scale ratings regress toward 0 at the reclassifier rate; negative ratings are retained unregressed. Improvement requires a separate verified 2026 source, entered with citation. |
| HFA | Default 2.5; neutral sites 0; team-specific overrides on TEAM RATINGS with reason/confidence/review date. Altitude, international, temporary stadiums, rivalry neutral sites, championships and extreme travel are handled as visible game adjustments, never hidden in team ratings. |
| Weekly snapshot | After clearing Data Quality, copy TEAM RATINGS Abbrev+Blended into HISTORY with the week number; the ±2.5 movement cap works against week-1-behind snapshots. Larger moves require a documented QB/coaching/roster/injury adjustment. |
| Win totals | VALIDATE-ONLY (PRESEASON AD/AE). Blending would require a deliberate, labeled settings change plus CHANGELOG entry — not built by default. |
| Overrides | Rating override: TEAM RATINGS col L (+reason+date). Margin/total override: ADJUSTMENTS rows targeting the GameID. Base calculations always remain visible; overrides are flagged on ENGINE/DASHBOARD and are reversible. |
| Google Sheets import checklist | After importing to native Google Sheets: File \> Settings \> Time zone = America/New\_York (drives TODAY/NOW-style behavior, as-of comparisons, line staleness and adjustment expiry) and Locale = United States. Then confirm AUDIT E1 = 0 and every invariant reads OK or pre-data N/A, and spot-check DASHBOARD values from the native Sheet — never from an offline Excel cache. Timezone is a per-spreadsheet Google Sheets setting; it does not travel inside the .xlsx file. |

| v0.6 | 2026-07-21 | GENUINE FORMULA DEFECT FOUND AND DOCUMENTED, NOT REPAIRED (per instruction to stop and report rather than silently fix): ADJUSTMENTS\!K6:K255 (Flags formula) - =IF($A6="","",IF($D6="","REASON MISSING; ","")\&IF(NOT(ISNUMBER($C6)),"VALUE NOT NUMERIC; ","")\&IF(AND(ISNUMBER($C6),ABS($C6)\>4,$A6\<\>"MARGIN OVERRIDE",$A6\<\>"TOTAL OVERRIDE"),"LARGE ADJ (\>4); ","")). Expected: a non-numeric Value (col C) shows a clean "VALUE NOT NUMERIC; " flag. Actual (confirmed with a real formula-calculation engine, and verified this is standard Excel/Sheets AND() eager-argument-evaluation behavior, not a verification-tool artifact): ABS($C6) is evaluated as an AND() argument even when ISNUMBER($C6) is FALSE, producing a \#VALUE\! error that propagates through the & concatenation and replaces the ENTIRE flag string with a raw error, corrupting DATA QUALITY\!B15's flag count for that row too. Proposed correction (not applied): replace the AND(...) term with IF(ISNUMBER($C6),AND(ABS($C6)\>4,$A6\<\>"MARGIN OVERRIDE",$A6\<\>"TOTAL OVERRIDE"),FALSE) so ABS() is never evaluated on a non-numeric value. No v0.6.1 was created; this workbook's ADJUSTMENTS formulas are byte-identical to approved v0.5.2. | v0.6 - DEFECT FOUND: ADJUSTMENTS\!K non-numeric-value \#VALUE\! propagation |
| v0.6 | 2026-07-21 | Related, non-defect behavioral finding: an oversized adjustment (e.g. 999 points, Active=Y) is NOT auto-blocked by any formula - it is flagged "LARGE ADJ (\>4); " in column K (informational only) but is still fully summed into the game's margin by ENGINE\!O (Manual adj). This matches the documented design ("manual review still required - never auto-clears") but is called out explicitly here since it may not be obvious from the UI alone: the weekly-review step (DATA QUALITY\!B15 / START HERE step 7) is the only safeguard, not an automatic cap. | v0.6 - confirmed design: adjustments flag, do not block, oversized entries |

| v0.6.1 | 2026-07-21 | SECOND GENUINE FORMULA DEFECT FOUND AND DOCUMENTED, NOT REPAIRED (found while completing the isolated DATA INCOMPLETE test this build requested; not authorized for repair this round): CLEAN\!C6:C1005 (week) and CLEAN\!D6:D1005 (date), pattern =IF($A6="","",'IMPORT SCHEDULE'\!C6) - when the source cell is genuinely blank, a bare cell reference evaluates to 0 in Excel/Sheets (confirmed with isolated formula probes matching standard spreadsheet semantics, not a verification-tool artifact), not "". Since ENGINE\!AI's DATA INCOMPLETE check is OR($B6="",$C6=""), and $B6/$C6 trace back through this pass-through, DATA INCOMPLETE can NEVER fire for a row with a valid GameID - it is unreachable dead code. A row with a genuinely missing week silently displays as Week 0 (indistinguishable from a real Week 0 game); a missing date silently displays the spreadsheet epoch date. Verified directly: temporarily blanking IMPORT SCHEDULE\!C8 (NC State @ Virginia's week field) still produced STATUS=READY, not DATA INCOMPLETE; the field was restored to its exact original value (0) immediately after. Proposed correction (documented only, not applied): nest an explicit blank check, e.g. CLEAN\!C6 -\> =IF($A6="","",IF('IMPORT SCHEDULE'\!C6="","",'IMPORT SCHEDULE'\!C6)), same pattern for CLEAN\!D6 - this would let a genuinely blank source propagate as "", which is all ENGINE\!AI's existing DATA INCOMPLETE check needs.
